- **correcting-repeated-mistakes**: Use when the same correction or review finding comes up a second time in a repo, when a finding is labelled repeat or follow-on, when a rule is already written down and agents still break it, or when asked to stop agents repeating a mistake. Finds the mistake class, fixes it at the highest level that works (architecture, then types, then a lint or CI check, then a test, then written instructions), proves the new check fails on the real past mistake, and records what now enforces the rule. Do not use it for a first-time mistake or a one-off bug; fix those and move on. ([cartwmic/dotfiles](https://github.com/cartwmic/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_local/share/agent-harness/canonical/skills/correcting-repeated-mistakes) · ★1)
